//! Candidate activation: project rows, validate invariants, build indexes
//! and derived rows, then publish the semantic generation in one commit.
// @ref LLP 3000.000#implementation-follow-up — one atomic activation boundary

pub(super) use super::activation_walk::walk;
use super::activation_walk::walk_metered;
use super::*;
use crate::ir::Program;
use crate::schema::{Constraint, materialize_optional_fields};
use crate::value::digest;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) type Identity = (Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>,
);

pub(super) fn identity(conn: &Connection) -> Result<Identity, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = conn.prepare_cached("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1")?;
    let mut get = |key| statement.query_row([key], |row| row.get(0)).optional();
    Ok((get("schema_hash")?, get("activation_hash")?, get("generation")?, get("device:generation")?,
    ))
}

impl Store {
    /// Install a device's schema. Server engines also supply their programs
    /// and derived-state builder through `activate` below.
    pub fn install(&mut self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.activate(schema, None, false, |_, _| Ok(())).map(|_| ())
    }

    /// A device can discard an obsolete partition while atomically caching
    /// its replacement backend and reset watermark. It never migrates rows
    /// whose authority belongs to a previous server generation.
    pub fn install_device(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked(schema, metadata, reset, &BTreeMap::new())
    }

    pub fn install_device_checked(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked_with(schema, metadata, reset, expected, |_| Ok(()))
    }

    /// Device-owned sidecar changes share the schema installation transaction.
    pub fn install_device_checked_with(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked_with_finish(schema, metadata, reset, expected, prepare, |_| {
            Ok(())
        })
    }

    /// Opening recovery and its final reads must finish before installation commits.
    pub fn install_device_checked_with_finish<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<R, RuntimeError> {
        let mut result = None;
        self.activate(schema, None, reset, |tx, _| {
            for (key, value) in expected {
                let held: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [key], |row| {
                        row.get(0)
                    }).optional()?;
                if &held != value { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the backend adoption capture is obsolete; retry sync".into())); }
            }
            prepare(tx)?;
            for (key, value) in metadata {
                // A device metadata namespace reset shares backend installation's transaction.
                if key.ends_with(':') {
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key >= ?1 AND key < ?2", params![key, format!("{key}\u{10ffff}")])?;
                } else { tx.set_meta(key, value)?; }
            }
            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _device_base; DELETE FROM _device_snapshot;")?; }
            result = Some(finish(tx)?);
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(result.expect("activation ran opening recovery"))
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_at(schema, programs, reset, crate::now_ms(), |tx, changed, _| build(tx, changed))
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate_at(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        // One-shot device preparation is never retried or given a larger cache.
        self.activate_transaction(schema, programs, reset, now, true, build)
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl Fn(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, mut build: impl FnMut(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl Fn(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        let budget = if programs.is_some() { super::activation_cache::configured_mib()? } else { 0 };
        // The server builder owns only transaction-local work. Recreate all of
        // it on retry, with the same schema/programs/activation time.
        self.with_activation_cache(budget, |store| {
            store.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, &check, &mut build, &finish)
        })
    }

    pub(super) fn activate_transaction(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, |_| Ok(()), build, |_| Ok(())).map(|(generation, ())| generation)
    }

    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — a removed opening boundary cannot lower durable source retirement time.
        let now=if self.server {now.max(self.meta("time_audience_clock")?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))}else{now};
        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
        if programs.is_some() {
            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
            }

                "SELECT c.name, COUNT(*) FROM i JOIN _catalog c ON c.num = i.ti WHERE length(i.k) > ?1 GROUP BY i.ti ORDER BY i.ti LIMIT 1",
                [crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i64], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?))
            ).optional()?;

                let (table, index) = name.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or((&name, "unknown"));

            }
        }

        let native: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_native_digest'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;

                    serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e,RuntimeError::Store))
                }).transpose()?;

        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#13-wave-13-preview-decisions — report interpreted literal fills from the committed candidate population.
        if let Some(previous) = &previous {
            for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
                let fills: Vec<_> = table.defaults.iter().filter_map(|(column, default)| {
                    let same_interpretation=previous.table(name).is_some_and(|old|
                        old.defaults.get(column)==Some(default) && old.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind|!kind.is_optional())
                        && !old.maintained.contains_key(column) && !old.engine_field(column));
                    if same_interpretation { return None; }
                    if table.columns[column].is_optional() || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || table.engine_field(column) { return None; }
                    match default { crate::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(value) => Some((column, value)), _ => None }
                }).collect();
                if fills.is_empty() || schema.maintain_for(name).is_some() || table.ephemeral.is_some() { continue; }
                let mut counts = vec![0u64; fills.len()];
                walk(&tx, catalog[name], |_, row| {
                    for (i,(column,_)) in fills.iter().enumerate() { if row.field(column).is_err() { counts[i] += 1; } }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                for ((column,value),count) in fills.into_iter().zip(counts).filter(|(_,count)| *count > 0) {
                    let value = match value { Value::String(text) => format!("'{}'", text.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\r', "\\r")), value => crate::wire::to_json(value).to_string() };
                    report.backfills.push(format!("{name}.{column} ← {value} on {count} {}",if count==1 {"row"} else {"rows"}));
                }
            }
        }
        let plans = super::activation_plan::plans(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
        if let Some(previous)=&previous {
            for step in crate::successor::classify(previous,schema) {
                let parts=step.path.split('.').collect::<Vec<_>>();
                let label=if parts.first()==Some(&"tables") && parts.len()>=2 {
                    let name=parts[1];
                    let label=super::adoption_report::table_label(if schema.table(name).is_some(){schema}else{previous},name);
                    if label==name && parts.get(2)==Some(&"columns") && parts.get(3).is_some_and(|c|!c.starts_with('$')) {format!("{label}.{}",parts[3])}else{label}
                } else if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
                    schema.maintains.get(position).map_or_else(||"view".into(),|m|m.label())
                } else {"backend".into()};
                let message=format!("{label} changed");
                if !report.changes.contains(&message){report.changes.push(message);}
            }
        }

        report.phase("preflight", &mut phase);
        if rebuilding {
            // @ref LLP 3000.000#implementation-follow-up — keys are still evaluated,
            // admitted and published in this transaction. Spill the key stream to
            // SQLite's temporary store, then insert in B-tree order instead of
            // repeatedly dirtying scattered main-database pages for every row.
            let ordered = programs.is_some() && plans.values().any(|plan| !plan.indexes.is_empty());
            #[cfg(test)]
            let ordered = ordered && !self.full_adoption;
            if ordered {
                super::activation_index::create(&tx)?;
            }
            // Removed names retain only their catalog number, never live row
            // images. Reintroducing a name starts empty, without resurrection.
            let active = super::activation_plan::active_names(schema);
            for (name, num) in &catalog {
                if !active.contains(name) {
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t = ?1", [num])?;
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1", [num])?;
                }
            }
            for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
                let t = catalog[name];
                let plan = &plans[name];
                // Launch put() already built these fresh indexes under the
                // complete candidate schema. Keep the final invariant pass,
                // but do not delete and reconstruct the same entries again.
                if launches.iter().any(|launch| {
                    schema.timelines.iter().any(|timeline| {
                        timeline.name == launch.name && ["Posts", "Work", "Edges", "Feed"].iter()
                        .any(|suffix| timeline.table(suffix) == *name)
                    })
                }) { continue; }
                if !plan.project && plan.indexes.is_empty() { continue; }
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                for index in &plan.indexes { tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti = ?1", [catalog[&format!("{name}.{index}")]])?; }
                let work = crate::rows::adoption::Work::table(table);
                let scratch = |row: &Value| crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(row, table);
                walk_metered(&tx, t, work, if work.index_entries != 0 { Some(&scratch) } else { None }, |id, mut row, contribution_meter| {
                    let keep = |column: &str| {
                            table.columns.contains_key(column) || (programs.is_none() && column == "pending")
                        };
                    let project = plan.project && row.as_object().is_ok_and(|fields| fields.keys().any(|column| !keep(column)));
                    // Persist only a destructive projection. Defaults and
                    // optionals are an interpretation of the stored image.
                    if project {
                        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.retain(|column, _| keep(column)); }
                        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = ?1 WHERE t = ?2 AND id = ?3", params![canonical_json(&row), t, id])?;
                    }
                    materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                    // A device's prediction mark is metadata, retained in
                    // storage but absent from the row the schema validates.
                    if programs.is_none() { if let Value::Row(fields) = &mut row { fields.remove("pending"); } }
                    // Maintained rows are replaced by the candidate builder.
                    if schema.maintain_for(name).is_none() { crate::engine::validate::validate_row(schema, name, &row)?; }
                    for index_name in &plan.indexes {
                        let index = &table.indexes[index_name];
                        let scratch = contribution_meter.mark();
                        let contribution = match index.components.first() { Some(crate::schema::IndexComponent::Contribution(m)) => Some(m), _ => None };
                        if let Some(m) = contribution {
                            contribution_meter.at(&format!("adopt {name}.{index_name}"));
                            contribution_meter.charge(crate::error::MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
                            contribution_meter.step(m.evaluation_steps())?;
                            crate::rows::adoption::charge(contribution_meter, crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(&row, table))?;
                        }
                        for key in index_keys(index, &row, id) {
                            if key.len() > crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES {
                                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(name, index_name, 1));
                            }
                            if contribution.is_some() { contribution_meter.charge(crate::error::MeterKind::IndexEntries, 1)?; }
                            super::activation_index::insert(&tx, ordered, catalog[&format!("{name}.{index_name}")], &key, id)?;
                        }
                        contribution_meter.release_heap(scratch);
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                let label = super::adoption_report::table_label(schema, name);
                report.detail(format!("project/index {label} ({} indexes)", plan.indexes.len()), started);
            }
            if ordered {
                let started = std::time::Instant::now();
                tx.conn().execute_batch("INSERT INTO main.i(ti, k, id) SELECT ti, k, id FROM temp._adoption_index ORDER BY ti, k; DROP TABLE temp._adoption_index;")?;
                report.detail("ordered index insertion", started);
            }
        }
        report.phase("project and index", &mut phase);
        // Upgrade old stores and rebuild only when the schema changes. Counts
        // participate in the same activation transaction as rows and indexes.
        if cap_counts && (population_changed || tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?.as_deref() != Some(&schema_hash)) {
            tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _cap_counts;")?;
            for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
                if table.is_expiring() && table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Cap { .. })) {
                    walk(&tx, catalog[name], |_, row| {
                        tx.update_cap_counts(name, table, None, Some(&row)).map(|_| ())
                    })?;
                }
            }
            tx.set_meta("cap_counts_schema", &schema_hash)?;
        }
        report.phase("cap counts", &mut phase);
        build(&tx, changed, &mut report)?;
        if rebuilding || reset || tx.meta(super::time_audience::PUBLICATION_STATE_KEY)?.as_deref() != Some("1") {
            tx.rebuild_time_audiences(schema, now)?;
        }
        report.phase("derived rebuild", &mut phase);
        if rebuilding && programs.is_some() && super::dormant::enabled(&tx)? {
            super::adopt_refusals::candidate(&tx,schema,if population_changed{None}else{previous.as_ref()},true)?;
        }
        if rebuilding {
            for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
                let plan = &plans[name];
                // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — physical reuse cannot exempt rebuilt rows from unchanged invariants.
                let rebuilt = schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| {
                    previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))
                });
                if plan.project || rebuilt {
                walk(&tx, catalog[name], |_, mut row| {
                    materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
                    if programs.is_none() { if let Value::Row(fields) = &mut row { fields.remove("pending"); } }
                    crate::engine::validate::validate_maintained_cache(schema, name, &row)?;
                    for (column, kind) in &table.columns { validate_assets(&tx, kind, row.field(column).ok())?; }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
                }
                for (index_name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(name, i)| {
                    i.unique && (plan.project || rebuilt || plan.indexes.contains(*name))
                }) {
                    validate_groups(&tx, name, index_name, index.components.len(), 1, true)?;
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.039#2.2 — a `set null` cap skips its null group; adding or removing that exemption re-validates the cap.
                let exemption_changed = previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|old| old.constraints.iter().any(|c|
                    matches!(c, Constraint::Cap { by, .. } if old.cap_exempts_null(by) != table.cap_exempts_null(by)))));
                for constraint in table.constraints.iter().filter(|_| plan.project || rebuilt || exemption_changed) {
                    if let Constraint::Cap { by, max } = constraint {
                        let (index_name, _) = table.indexes.iter().find(|(_, i)| i.is_plain() && i.columns().starts_with(by)).ok_or_else(|| {
                                RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name} cap has no matching index"))
                            })?;
                        validate_groups(&tx, name, index_name, by.len(), u64::from(*max), table.cap_exempts_null(by))?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        report.phase("validation", &mut phase);
        if rebuilding {
            // A removed asset home is retired in the same transaction as its
            // projection. The ordinary collector removes bytes after commit.
            let homes: Vec<(String, String)> = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT home_table, home_column FROM _assets WHERE home_table IS NOT NULL AND released_at IS NULL")?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
            for (table, column) in homes {
                if schema.table(&table).and_then(|t| t.columns.get(&column)).is_none_or(|kind| !contains_asset_type(kind)) && !super::dormant::holds_home(&tx,&table,&column)? {
                    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at = ?3 WHERE home_table = ?1 AND home_column = ?2 AND released_at IS NULL", params![table, column, crate::now_ms()])?;
                }
            }
        }
        let previous = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation'", [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let generation = if programs.is_some() && changed { previous.checked_add(1).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))? } else { previous.max(1) };
        tx.set_meta("schema_hash", &schema_hash)?;
        if let Some(hash) = &activation_hash {
            tx.set_meta("index_keys_validated", &format!("{}:{hash}", crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES))?;
        } else {
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [])?;
        }
        if let (Some(hash), Some(programs)) = (&activation_hash, programs) {
            tx.set_meta("activation_hash", hash)?;
            tx.set_meta("generation", &generation.to_string())?;
            if changed {
                tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _generations(generation, schema, programs, at) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)", params![i64::try_from(generation).map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))?, canonical_json(schema), canonical_json(programs), crate::now_ms()])?;
            }
            // Inspection-only metadata shares the successful activation's
            // transaction, but never its schema bytes, hash or generation.
            if let Some(indexes) = &schema.authored_indexes.0 {
                let record = crate::schema::inspection::Record {
                    generation, schema_hash, indexes: indexes.clone(),
                };
                tx.set_meta(crate::schema::inspection::META_KEY, &canonical_json(&record))?;
            }
        }
        let after = identity(tx.conn())?;
        super::live_activation::finalize(&tx)?;
        let result = finish(&tx)?;
        super::live_activation::cleanup(&tx)?;
        report.phase("metadata", &mut phase);
        tx.commit()?;
        report.phase("commit", &mut phase);
        // Reopening an unchanged backend must not announce a new adoption.
        self.adoption_report = if changed || reset { report } else { Default::default() };
        // Historical ordinals stay in _catalog, but no runtime transaction can address memory tables.
        catalog.retain(|name,_| {
            !schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,t)| {
                t.ephemeral.is_some() && (name == table || name.starts_with(&format!("{table}.")))
            })
        });
        self.catalog = catalog;
        self.identity = after;
        self.accept_open();
        Ok((generation, result))
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — a key-bound proof must be invalidated even by direct SQL edits.
fn index_key_guards(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let limit = crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES;
    let insert = format!("_index_keys_insert_{limit}");
    let update = format!("_index_keys_update_{limit}");
    let installed: i64 = conn.query_row(
        "SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type = 'trigger' AND name IN (?1, ?2)",
        params![insert, update], |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    if installed != 2 {
        // A predecessor without both guards could have retained a proof across
        // physical edits. Install and revalidate in this activation transaction.
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [])?;
        conn.execute_batch(&format!(
            "CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS {insert} AFTER INSERT ON i WHEN length(NEW.k) > {limit}
             BEGIN DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'; END;
             CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS {update} AFTER UPDATE OF k ON i WHEN length(NEW.k) > {limit}
             BEGIN DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'; END;"
        ))?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Equal prefixes are adjacent in an index. Validate uniqueness and caps
/// in one bounded pass per invariant, retaining one key rather than one
/// allocation for every group/row in a potentially large activation.
fn validate_groups(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, index: &str, prefix_len: usize, max: u64, nulls_distinct: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    use crate::{error::MeterKind, meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter}, rows::word_heap as heap};
    // Only encoded group keys matter. Fetching source images here would
    // recreate an unmetered 500-row payload buffer after the metered rebuild.
    let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT k FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 ORDER BY k")?;
    let mut rows = statement.query([tx.num(&format!("{table}.{index}"))?])?;
    let mut previous: Option<Vec<Value>> = None;
    let mut count = 0;
    let mut visited = 0;
    let mut meter = Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort);
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        if visited % 256 == 0 {
            meter = Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort);
            meter.at(&format!("adopt {table}.{index} groups"));
            meter.once(crate::meter::HeapSite::SqliteColumns, 2 * tx.page_size)?;
        }
        visited += 1;
        let scratch = meter.mark();
        meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
        let key = row.get_ref(0)?.as_blob().map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        meter.heap(heap::key_decode(key.len(), key.len()))?;
        if let Some(values) = &previous {
            meter.heap(heap::slots::<Value>(values.len()))?;
            crate::engine::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut meter, |meter, scope| {
                for value in values { value.retained_internal(scope).charge(meter, scope)?; }
                Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(())
            })?;
        }
        let mut group = crate::key::decode(key).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("invalid rebuilt index key".into()))?;
        group.truncate(prefix_len);
        if !(nulls_distinct && group.contains(&Value::Null)) {
            if previous.as_ref() == Some(&group) { count += 1; } else { previous = Some(group); count = 1; }
            if count > max { return Err(RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("cannot activate {table}.{index}: existing group exceeds {}", if nulls_distinct { "a unique key".to_owned() } else { format!("cap {max}") }))); }
        }
        meter.release_heap(scratch);
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn contains_asset_type(kind: &crate::schema::ColumnType) -> bool {
    use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;
    match kind { Optional(inner) | List { of: inner, .. } | ListRange { of: inner, .. } => {
            contains_asset_type(inner)
        }
        Image { .. } | Video { .. } | File { .. } => true, _ => false }
}
pub(super) fn validate_assets(tx: &Tx<'_>, kind: &crate::schema::ColumnType, value: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    use crate::assets::AssetKind;
    use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;
    match (kind, value) {
        (Optional(inner), Some(value)) => validate_assets(tx, inner, Some(value)),
        (List { of, .. } | ListRange { of, .. }, Some(Value::Array(values))) => { for value in values { validate_assets(tx, of, Some(value))?; } Ok(()) }
        (Image { .. } | Video { .. } | File { .. }, Some(value)) => {
            let (wanted, max, types) = kind.asset_leaf().expect("an asset leaf");
            if let Some(id) = crate::assets::asset_id(Some(value)) {
                if let Some(asset) = tx.asset(id)? {
                    if asset.kind != wanted { return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("cannot activate: {id} is {}, not {}", asset.kind.word(), wanted.word()) }); }
                    if wanted == AssetKind::File && !crate::assets::file_accepts(types, &asset.content_type) { return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("cannot activate: {id} is {}, which the column no longer accepts", asset.content_type) }); }
                    if asset.bytes > u64::from(max) { return Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", message: format!("cannot activate: {id} has {} bytes; the column takes at most {max}", asset.bytes) }); }
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(())
    }
}

   189	fn add_reference(
   190	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   191	    t: i64,
   192	    id: &str,
   193	    column: &str,
   194	    parent: &str,
   195	    parent_id: &str,
   196	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   197	    let parent_t = tx.num(parent)?;
   198	    let invalid = !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
   199	        && !tx.conn().query_row(
   200	            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   201	            params![parent_t, parent_id],
   202	            |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0),
   203	        )?;
   204	    tx.conn().execute(
   205	        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",
   206	        params![t, id, column, parent_t, parent_id, if invalid {2} else {0}],
   207	    )?;
   208	    Ok(())
   209	}
   210	pub(super) fn capture_through_parent(
   211	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   212	    name: &str,
   213	    table: &Table,
   214	    id: &str,
   215	    column: &str,
   216	    row: &Value,
   217	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   218	    if let Some(through) = &table.through {
   219	        if (through.fields.contains_key(column)
   220	            || matches!(
   221	                column,
   222	                crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE | crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
   223	            ))
   224	            && let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference)
   225	        {
   226	            add_reference(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, &through.parent, parent_id)?;
   227	        }
   228	    }
   229	    Ok(())
   230	}

   757	pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   758	    super::backfills::delete(tx,t,id)?;
   759	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   760	        return Ok(());
   761	    }
   762	    tx.conn().execute(
   763	        "UPDATE _dormant_refs SET invalid=1 WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2 AND invalid=0",
   764	        params![t, id],
   765	    )?;
   766	    let narrowed=super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())?;
   767	    let home=if narrowed {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2)"} else {""};
   768	    tx.conn().execute(&format!("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?3) WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_id=?2 AND (home_column IN (SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2){home})"),params![name,id,crate::now_ms(),t])?;
   769	    tx.conn().execute(
   770	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   771	        params![t, id],
   772	    )?;
   773	    tx.conn().execute(
   774	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   775	        params![t, id],
   776	    )?;
   777	    if narrowed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;}
   778	    tx.conn().execute(
   779	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   780	        params![t, id],
   781	    )?;
   782	    Ok(())
   783	}
   784	
   785	pub(super) fn holds_home(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, column: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   786	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   787	        return Ok(false);
   788	    }
   789	    let narrowed=if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON n.t=c.num WHERE c.name=?1)"}else{""};
   790	    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2){narrowed}"),params![table,column],|r|r.get(0))?)
   791	}
   792	
   793	/// Fetch only destination cells on live transitions, after paying their full
   794	/// serialized/decoded coexistence in the owning activation page/row account.
   795	pub(super) fn restored_values(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,meter:&mut crate::meter::Meter)->Result<Vec<(String,String)>,RuntimeError> {
   796	    if !super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   797	        return Ok(tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?);
   798	    }
   799	    let mut values=Vec::new();
   800	    for column in table.columns.keys() {
   801	        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   802	        if let Some(bytes)=bytes {
   803	            meter.heap((bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(1024+column.len() as u64*8))?;
   804	            let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
   805	            values.push((column.clone(),data));
   806	        }
   807	    }
   808	    Ok(values)
   809	}
   810	
   811	/// Narrowed forward correspondence has already run when this is called.
   812	/// Record cell-derived live provenance before consuming any source evidence.
   813	pub(super) fn mark_live_column(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
   814	    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? && super::authority_origin::mode(tx,t,id)? != Some(1) {
   815	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',?1,'',?2 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=1)",params![t,id,column])?;
   816	    }Ok(())
   817	}

//! Human preview groups facts about one affected table/operation once.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#15-wave-15-preview-and-rollback-day5 — full evidence remains available in JSON and verbose output.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::{Schema, ir::Program, store::DormantItem, successor::Step};
struct Group { warnings: Vec<String>, notes: Vec<String>, priority: u8 }
impl Default for Group { fn default() -> Self {Self {warnings:Vec::new(),notes:Vec::new(),priority:2}} }
fn table_prefix(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
    tables.iter().filter(|name|text.strip_prefix(name.as_str()).is_some_and(|tail|tail.is_empty() || tail.starts_with(['.',':',' ']))).max_by_key(|name|name.len()).cloned()
}
fn subject(text: &str, tables: &BTreeSet<String>, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>) -> String {
    // Read only generated subject positions, never arbitrary words inside an
    // authored predicate/refusal/value. Unknown formats remain release facts.
    if let Some(table)=table_prefix(text,tables) {return table;}
    for marker in ["table ","dependent ","backfill: "] {
        if let Some(tail)=text.strip_prefix(marker) && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    let prefix=text.split(';').next().unwrap_or(text);
    if text.starts_with("queries/counts ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" read ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("release ") && let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" safety dependencies on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    if text.starts_with("rollback users get release ") {
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once(" quotas on ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
        if let Some((_,tail))=prefix.split_once("'s ") && let Some(table)=table_prefix(tail,tables) {return table;}
    }
    if text.starts_with("release ") {
        if let Some(token)=prefix.split_whitespace().last() && let Some((owner,effect))=token.rsplit_once('.') {
            if effect=="require" {return format!("operation {owner}");}
            if tables.contains(owner) && matches!(effect,"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete") {return owner.into();}
        }
        for name in programs.keys() {
            if prefix.ends_with(&format!(" operation {name}")) {return format!("operation {name}");}
        }
    }
    "release behavior".into()
}
fn brief(text: &str) -> String {
    // Semicolons also separate authored guards and multiple cap declarations;
    // retaining the full fact avoids dropping their contracts or caveats.
    text.to_owned()
}
pub(super) fn print(current: Option<&Schema>, target: &Schema, programs: &BTreeMap<String,Program>, warnings: &[String], notes: &[String], steps: &[Step], items: &[DormantItem]) {
    let tables: BTreeSet<_>=target.tables.keys().chain(current.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.tables.keys())).cloned().collect();
    let mut groups=BTreeMap::<String,Group>::new();
    for (warning, lines) in [(true,warnings),(false,notes)] {
        for line in lines {
            let group=groups.entry(subject(line,&tables,programs)).or_default();
            let priority=if line.contains("safety guard") || line.contains(".require;") {0} else if line.contains("confidentiality") || line.contains("safety rule") {1} else {2};
            group.priority=group.priority.min(priority);
            let text=if priority==0 {line.clone()} else {brief(line)};
            let dest=if warning {&mut group.warnings} else {&mut group.notes};
            if !dest.contains(&text) {dest.push(text);}
        }
    }
    for step in steps {
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path);
        let key=table_prefix(path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||path.to_owned());
        if path.strip_prefix(&key).is_some_and(|tail|tail.starts_with(".indexes.")) {continue;}
        let group=groups.entry(key).or_default();
        // Keep each user-visible source change even when another fact already
        // names this table. Only index plumbing is verbose-only.
        let detail=brief(&format!("{}: {}",path.replace(".columns.","."),step.message));
        if !group.notes.contains(&detail) {group.notes.push(detail);}
    }
    let mut storage=BTreeMap::<String,[u64;3]>::new();
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.rows>0 && item.kind!="backfill") {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        let slot=match item.kind.as_str() {"column"=>1,"index"=>2,_=>0};
        storage.entry(name).or_default()[slot]+=item.rows;
    }
    for (name,[rows,cells,indexes]) in storage {
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {rows} dormant rows, {cells} cells, {indexes} index entries"));
    }
    for item in items.iter().filter(|item|item.kind=="backfill" && item.rows>0) {
        let name=table_prefix(&item.path,&tables).unwrap_or_else(||item.path.clone());
        groups.entry(name).or_default().notes.push(format!("retained: {} private backfill preimages ({} bytes)",item.rows,item.bytes));
    }
    let mut groups=groups.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    groups.sort_by_key(|(name,g)|(g.priority,name.clone()));
    for (name,mut group) in groups {
        let warning=!group.warnings.is_empty();
        group.warnings.append(&mut group.notes);
        println!("  {} {name}: {}{}",if warning {"WARNING"} else {"note:"},group.warnings.join("; "),if warning {"; verify these cases or preserve the current behavior in a bridge release"} else {""});
    }
}

#[test]
fn authored_values_cannot_assign_a_guard_to_an_unrelated_table() {
    let tables=BTreeSet::from(["unrelated".to_owned(),"workspace.plans".to_owned()]);
    let line="release 1 loosens book.require; old: require title != 'unrelated' else STOP; new: allow; rollback users lose a current safety guard";
    assert_eq!(subject(line,&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"operation book");
    assert_eq!(subject("workspace.plans.state: 2 rows become dormant",&tables,&BTreeMap::new()),"workspace.plans");
}

    17	fn incompatible_except(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema,name:&str,row:&Value,authority:&[String])->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    18	    let table = &schema.tables[name];
    19	    for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
    20	        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || authority.contains(column) {continue;}
    21	        if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
    22	            if incompatible_column(tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? { return Ok(true); }
    23	        }
    24	    }
    25	    Ok(false)
    26	}

    28	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — the warning and activation share the value predicate, including ledger-backed assets.
    29	pub(super) fn incompatible_column(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &Table, name: &str, column: &str, kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    30	    if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name, column, kind, Some(value)).is_err() { return Ok(true); }
    31	    // @ref LLP 3000.038#1-the-type — asset facts belong to the ledger, including optional/list leaves.
    32	    match super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, Some(value)) {
    33	        Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE" | "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", .. }) => return Ok(true),
    34	        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    35	        Ok(()) => (),
    36	    }
    37	    Ok(table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { field, min, format }
    38	        if field == column && matches!(value, Value::String(s)
    39	            if crate::engine::validate::validate_text(field, s, *min, *format).is_err()))))
    40	}
    41	
    42	fn refs(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    43	    match (kind, value) {
    44	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), v) => refs(inner, v, out),
    45	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(vs)) => {
    46	            for v in vs {
    47	                refs(of, v, out);
    48	            }
    49	        }
    50	        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
    51	        _ => {}
    52	    }
    53	}

    55	pub(super) fn transition(
    56	    tx: &Tx<'_>,

    64	    // Backfill sources already retired through the ordinary action closure,
    65	    // before patching. Never re-test predecessor deadlines on transformed rows.
    66	    if next.backfills.is_empty() && !super::backfills::has_evidence(tx)? {retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;}

    68	        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows)",

    76	                    .iter()

    78	                    || t.constraints

    81	                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    82	                        != p.constraints

    90	        return Ok(());

    95	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    96	    // References to a hidden row hide their dependents, including memberships.
    97	    loop {

   112	                crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(
   113	                    &mut row,

   115	                );
   116	                let mut hidden = incompatible(tx,next, name, &row)?;
   117	                for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
   118	                    let mut edges = Vec::new();

   169	                        let captured:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
   170	                        if captured {

   172	                        }

   175	                        }

   177	                        // Nominal keep references may already be dangling. They have no bound lifetime.
   178	                    }
   179	                }
   180	                tx.conn()

   182	                removed += 1;
   183	                Ok(())
   184	            })?;
   185	            tx.clear_activation_scan();
   186	        }
   187	        if removed == 0 {
   188	            break;
   189	        }
   190	    }
   191	    // Consume only rows that survived the closed population, not hidden dependents.
   192	    let restored:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;

   213	pub(super) fn reserved(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   214	    if !present(tx.conn())? {
   215	        return Ok(false);

   270	pub(super) fn restore_images(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,now:i64,source_definitions:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,authority_sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,for_backfills:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   271	    let rewritten=if for_backfills {super::backfills::columns(tx,next)?}else{Default::default()};

   276	            params![cursor.0,cursor.1],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?))).optional()?;
   277	        let Some((t,id,data_bytes,definition_bytes))=item else {break};

   281	        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   282	            admission.heap((data_bytes as u64).saturating_add(definition_bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;

   287	            tx.conn()
   288	                .query_row("SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=?1", [t], |r| r.get(0))?;
   289	        let Some(table) = next
   290	            .table(&name)
   291	            .filter(|t| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(&name).is_none())
   292	        else {
   293	            continue;
   294	        };
   295	        let old: Table =
   296	            serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   297	        super::dormant::check_owner(tx, &name, &old, next)?;
   298	        let mut row: Value =
   299	            serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   300	        super::backfills::record_omissions(tx,t,&id,&row)?;

   302	        if for_backfills && old.expired(&row,now) {
   303	            // Expose an expired source only inside the uncommitted lifecycle
   304	            // phase so the ordinary engine can close its reference actions.
   305	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
   306	            continue;
   307	        }
   308	        let mut authority=crate::engine::through_activation::manual_authority_fields(next,&name,&old);
   309	        if for_backfills { authority.extend(rewritten.get(&name).into_iter().flatten().cloned()); }
   310	        if incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)? {
   311	            continue;
   312	        }
   313	        // Retained source deadlines precede projection, including removal of read-through itself.
   314	        if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,&old,&row,now)  {
   315	            tx.delete(&name,&old,&row)?;
   316	            continue;
   317	        }
   318	        // Actual deletion/reuse never recaptures a different parent lifetime.
   319	        let invalid_columns = tx
   320	            .conn()
   321	            .prepare_cached(
   322	                "SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND invalid=1",
   323	            )?

   325	            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
   326	        let mut invalid=false;
   327	        for c in &invalid_columns {
   328	            if for_backfills && next.backfills.iter().any(|f| f.table==name && f.inputs.contains_key(c)) {
   329	                return Err(RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:format!("backfill historical reference {name}.{c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")});
   330	            }
   331	            if table.columns.contains_key(c) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,c,table,Some(&old))? {invalid=true;}
   332	        }
   333	        if invalid {
   334	            continue;
   335	        }
   336	        if super::authority_origin::live_row(tx,t,&id,&old,table)? {
   337	            admission.heap(crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,source_definitions,&name,&old).saturating_add(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_mul(4)))?;
   338	        }
   339	        super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,&id,&old)?;
   340	        super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now,true)?;
   341	        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_image(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now)?;
   342	        if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored(kind,t,col,id) VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;}
   343	        for column in old.columns.keys() {
   344	            if !table.columns.contains_key(column) {
   345	                super::dormant::definition(tx, &name, &old, column)?;
   346	                if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
   347	                    super::dormant::save_cell(tx, &name, &old, &id, column, value, false)?;
   348	                }
   349	            }
   350	        }
   351	        if !for_backfills { if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
   352	            fields.retain(|c, _| table.columns.contains_key(c));
   353	        }}
   354	        let values=super::dormant::restored_values(tx,t,&id,table,&mut admission)?;
   355	        super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,&id,Some(&old),table,&mut row,values)?;
   356	        if old.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some()) {
   357	            for column in table.columns.keys() {super::dormant::mark_live_column(tx,t,&id,column)?;}
   358	        }
   359	        crate::engine::through_activation::manual_image(tx,next,&name,&old,&mut row,now)?;
   360	        if if for_backfills {incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)?}else{incompatible(tx,next,&name,&row)?} {continue;}
   361	        if !for_backfills {crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);}
   362	        // Missing required new fields are a genuine backfill refusal, not dormancy.
   363	        tx.conn().execute(
   364	            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
   365	            params![t, id, canonical_json(&row)],
   366	        )?;
   367	    }
   368	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
   369	    Ok(())
   370	}

#[test]
fn physically_sparse_defaults_are_interpreted_and_receipts_keep_that_interpretation() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let defaulted=old.replace("  public","  n: int default 3\n  public");
  let fill=format!("backfill items twice(n: {kind}):\n  {{doubled:(n ?? 0) * 2}}\n");
  let next=defaulted.replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:")+&fill;
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a"}));adopt(&mut e,&defaulted,2).unwrap();
  assert!(row(&mut e,"items","a").field("n").is_err(),"fixture is physically sparse");
  adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
  let later=next.replace("default 3","default 100");adopt(&mut e,&later,4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",5).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,4).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));
  let rollback=later.replace("  doubled: int\n","").replace(&fill,"");adopt(&mut e,&rollback,6).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,4).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6),"rollback/forward keeps the original omitted-input interpretation");
 }
}

#[test]
fn saved_generic_id_binding_survives_type_erasure_and_multiple_restorations() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  parent:","  copied: int\n  parent:")+"backfill children copy(parent: id):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let erased=next.replace("parent: parents on delete keep","parent: id");
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap();
 adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();
 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
 seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));let error=adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn reentering_omission_cohorts_do_not_change_a_new_declarations_source_snapshot() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let defaulted=old.replace("  public","  n: int default 3\n  public");
 let fill="backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let next=defaulted.replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:")+fill;
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a"}));adopt(&mut e,&defaulted,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();
 let rollback=defaulted.replace("default 3","default 100");adopt(&mut e,&rollback,4).unwrap();
 let fresh=next.replace("default 3","default 100").replace("  doubled:","  fresh: int\n  doubled:")+"backfill items current(n: int):\n  {fresh:n * 2}\n";
 adopt(&mut e,&fresh,5).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","fresh"),Value::Int(200));
}

#[test]
fn set_null_expiry_updates_a_column_archived_during_that_release() {
 let parent="table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let child="table children:\n  parent: parents ? on delete set null\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let item="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=format!("{parent}{child}{item}");let next=old.replace("  parent: parents ? on delete set null\n","").replace("  cap 4 by parent\n","").replace("  n:","  doubled: int\n  n:")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
 let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":50})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"})),("items",json!({"id":"i","n":3}))];
 let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
 let(s,p)=compile(&next);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,100).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);
 let fresh=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["parents"],&json!({"id":"p","until":500})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("parents".into(),fresh)],100).unwrap();
 let(s,p)=compile(&old);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,Some(2)),false,101).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","parent"),Value::Null);
}

fn inactive_generic_id_receipts_refuse_a_reused_parent_before_any_lookup() {

 let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));

#[test]
fn historical_lifetime_outputs_refuse_before_transforming_or_retiring_children() {
 for action in ["cascade","set null","restrict"] {
  let optional=if action=="set null" {" ?"}else{""};
  let old=format!("table parents:\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents{optional} on delete {action}\n  cap 4 by parent\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let next=old.replace("  expire at .until\n","")+"backfill parents shorten(until: time):\n  {until:until - 1d}\n";
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let rows=[("parents",json!({"id":"p","until":500})),("children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}))];let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|(t,r)|(t.to_owned(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[t],&r).unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>();e.seed_at(rows,0).unwrap();
  let generation=e.generation();let(s,p)=compile(&next);let error=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,100).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lifetime"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.generation(),generation);assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"parents","p","until"),Value::Int(500));assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),1);
 }
}
#[test]
fn computed_text_minimum_and_format_failures_refuse_the_whole_adoption() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 for (kind,value,reason) in [("text 2..30","' '","non-whitespace"),("text 2..30 format handle","'Not A Handle'","handle"),("text <=80 format email","'bad@@example.test'","email")] {
  let next=old.replace("  n:",&format!("  label: {kind}\n  n:"))+&format!("backfill items label():\n  {{label:{value}}}\n");
  let mut e=engine(old);for id in ["a","b"]{seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":id,"n":3}));}let generation=e.generation();let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains(reason)&&error.contains("2 affected rows"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.generation(),generation);assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),2);assert!(row(&mut e,"items","a").field("label").is_err());
 }
}

#[test]
fn widened_narrowed_rows_capture_their_original_reference_argument_types() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  parent: parents on delete keep\n  n: int 0..10\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("parent: parents on delete keep","parent: id").replace("int 0..10","int 0..5");
 let wide=narrow.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10").replace("  parent: id","  copied: int\n  parent: id")+"backfill children copy(parent: id):\n  {copied:(parents[parent] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","parent":"p","n":9}));adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);adopt(&mut e,&wide,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","copied"),Value::Int(3));
 adopt(&mut e,parents,4).unwrap();let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","n":77}));
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&wide,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn widened_narrowed_rows_cannot_rewrite_their_original_lifetime_fields() {
 for (clause,field,value) in [("expire at .until","until",500),("not before .opens","opens",0)] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int 0..10\n  {field}: time\n  {clause}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let narrow=old.replace("int 0..10","int 0..5").replace(&format!("  {clause}\n"),"");
  let next=narrow.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10")+&format!("backfill items move({field}: time):\n  {{{field}:{field} + 1d}}\n");
  let(s,p)=compile(&old);let mut e=Engine::new_release_prepared_at(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(1,None),false,0).unwrap();
  let input=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["items"],&json!({"id":"a","n":9,field:value})).unwrap();e.seed_at([("items".into(),input)],0).unwrap();
  let(s,p)=compile(&narrow);e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(2,Some(1)),false,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
  let(s,p)=compile(&next);let error=e.adopt_release_at(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(2)),false,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lifetime"),"{error}");assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 }
}

#[test]
fn restored_output_cells_keep_preimages_across_rollback_and_table_suspension() {
 for kind in ["int","int ?"] {
  let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  score: {kind}\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n");
  let dropped=old.replace(&format!("  score: {kind}\n"),"");let next=old.clone()+"backfill items score(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2}\n";
  let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3,"score":7}));adopt(&mut e,&dropped,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(6));
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(7));adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(7));adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(6));
 }
}
#[test]
fn replacing_added_output_mappings_restores_each_version_instead_of_a_default() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.replace("  n:","  score: int default 0\n  n:")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2}\n";let triple=twice.replace("twice(n:","triple(n:").replace("n * 2","n * 3");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(9));
 adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(6));adopt(&mut e,&triple,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(9));
}

#[test]
fn partially_hidden_transformed_fields_resume_their_version_and_undo_on_plain_return() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  score: int\n  other: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
 let fill="backfill items pair(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2,other:n * 3}\n";
 let next=old.to_owned()+fill+"mutation edit():\n  update items['a'] {score:11}\n";
 let hidden=old.replace("  score: int\n","");
 for edited in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3,"score":7,"other":8}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();if edited {write(&mut e,"edit");}
  adopt(&mut e,&hidden,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","other"),Value::Int(8));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(if edited {11}else{6}));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","other"),Value::Int(9));
  adopt(&mut e,&hidden,3).unwrap();
  if edited {let error=adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("changed after backfill"),"{error}");}
  else {adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(7));}
 }
}
#[test]
fn newly_transformed_reference_cells_keep_binding_when_the_old_row_definition_lacked_them() {
 let parents="table parents:\n  name: text <=10\n  n: int\n  unique name\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=parents.to_owned()+"table children:\n  rank: int 0..10\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let link="backfill children link():\n  {link:(parents[name = 'p'] ! else NOT_FOUND).id}\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("  rank:","  link: parents\n  rank:").replace("int 0..10","int 0..5")+link;
 let erased=narrow.replace("  link: parents","  link: id");let wide=erased.replace("int 0..5","int 0..10").replace("  rank:","  copied: int\n  rank:")+"backfill children copy(link: id):\n  {copied:(parents[link] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(&old);seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","name":"p","n":3}));seed(&mut e,"children",json!({"id":"c","rank":9}));adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&erased,3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&wide,4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"children","c","copied"),Value::Int(3));
 adopt(&mut e,parents,5).unwrap();let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();{let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let r=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&r).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}seed(&mut e,"parents",json!({"id":"p","name":"p","n":77}));
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&wide,4).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("parent lifetime"),"{error}");
}

#[test]
fn inverse_absence_does_not_reuse_a_restored_output_cell_or_its_default() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let first=old.replace("  n:","  score: int default 10\n  n:")+"backfill items first(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2}\n";
 let second=old.replace("  n:","  score: int default 10\n  n:")+"backfill items second(score: int ?):\n  {score:(score ?? 0) + 1}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&first,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&second,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(1));
 let plain=old.replace("  n:","  score: int default 10\n  n:");adopt(&mut e,&plain,4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&second,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(1));
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",5).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&second,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(1));
 adopt(&mut e,&plain,4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(10));
}
#[test]
fn existing_later_cohorts_see_replayed_earlier_values_after_old_release_writes() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nmutation edit():\n  update items['a'] {n:4}\n";
 let first=old.replace("  n:","  score: int\n  n:")+"backfill items first(n: int):\n  {score:n * 2}\n";
 let second=first.replace("  n:","  copied: int\n  n:")+"backfill items second(score: int):\n  {copied:score}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&first,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&second,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(6));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");adopt(&mut e,&second,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","score"),Value::Int(8));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(8));
}

    44	            Ok(())

    47	
    48	    /// Opening recovery and its final reads must finish before installation commits.
    49	    pub fn install_device_checked_with_finish<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    50	    ) -> Result<R, RuntimeError> {
    51	        let mut result = None;
    52	        self.activate(schema, None, reset, |tx, _| {
    53	            for (key, value) in expected {
    54	                let held: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [key], |row| {
    55	                        row.get(0)
    56	                    }).optional()?;
    57	                if &held != value { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the backend adoption capture is obsolete; retry sync".into())); }
    58	            }
    59	            prepare(tx)?;
    60	            for (key, value) in metadata {
    61	                // A device metadata namespace reset shares backend installation's transaction.
    62	                if key.ends_with(':') {
    63	                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key >= ?1 AND key < ?2", params![key, format!("{key}\u{10ffff}")])?;
    64	                } else { tx.set_meta(key, value)?; }
    65	            }
    66	            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _device_base; DELETE FROM _device_snapshot;")?; }
    67	            result = Some(finish(tx)?);
    68	            Ok(())
    69	        })?;
    70	        Ok(result.expect("activation ran opening recovery"))
    71	    }

    73	    pub(crate) fn activate(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    74	        self.activate_at(schema, programs, reset, crate::now_ms(), |tx, changed, _| build(tx, changed))

    77	    pub(crate) fn activate_at(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    78	        // One-shot device preparation is never retried or given a larger cache.
    79	        self.activate_transaction(schema, programs, reset, now, true, build)
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl Fn(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, mut build: impl FnMut(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl Fn(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    83	    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
    84	        let budget = if programs.is_some() { super::activation_cache::configured_mib()? } else { 0 };
    85	        // The server builder owns only transaction-local work. Recreate all of
    86	        // it on retry, with the same schema/programs/activation time.
    87	        self.with_activation_cache(budget, |store| {
    88	            store.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, &check, &mut build, &finish)
    89	        })
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    pub(super) fn activate_transaction(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    93	        self.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, |_| Ok(()), build, |_| Ok(())).map(|(generation, ())| generation)
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    97	    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
    98	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — a removed opening boundary cannot lower durable source retirement time.
    99	        let now=if self.server {now.max(self.meta("time_audience_clock")?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))}else{now};
   100	        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
   101	        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
   102	        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
   103	        if programs.is_some() {
   104	            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
   105	                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
   106	            }
   107	            let estimates = self.check_timeline_activation_at(schema, now, allow_launch)?;
   108	            for estimate in estimates.iter().filter(|e| e.sources != 0) { crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic feed adoption: {}", estimate.summary()); }
   109	        }
   110	        report.phase("read-only advice", &mut phase);
   111	        self.check_ownership()?;
   112	        let sql = self.conn.transaction_with_behavior(rusqlite::TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
   113	        // Low-level preparation may precede a writer-lock wait. Recheck all
   114	        // opening admission under the lock that owns the upgrades.
   115	        Self::check_version(&sql)?;
   116	        let before = identity(&sql)?;
   117	        if before != self.identity { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("another engine activated this store; reopen it before activating source".into())); }
   118	        check(&sql)?;
   119	        // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — physical upgrades roll back with adoption.
   120	        // Rollback restores schema/rows and freelist count. It may leave
   121	        // changed free-page contents or journal/WAL residue, not committed data.
   122	        Self::initialize_schema(&sql)?;
   123	        let now=if self.server {
   124	            now.max(sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='time_audience_clock'",[],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))
   125	        }else{now};
   126	        if programs.is_some() { Self::initialize_change_evidence(&sql)?; }
   127	        index_key_guards(&sql)?;
   128	        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — reuse an admitted key bound, never assume a legacy store was checked.
   129	        // Physical INSERT/UPDATE triggers invalidate the proof on oversized keys,
   130	        // including direct SQL. Every activation key
   131	        // below is checked too. Bind the proof to the predecessor so an older

impl Shared {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — current adopted auth policy closes test authority, independently of dev mode.
    pub(crate) fn dev_doors(&self) -> bool {
        if !self.personas { return false; }
        let bound = lock(&self.bound);
        self.unsafe_dev_doors || !(bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
    }
    // Revalidate under the writer: neither a slow body nor an adoption queue
    // may carry fixture authority into a newly password-only schema.
    pub(crate) fn dev_authority(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
        if self.personas && (self.unsafe_dev_doors || !(engine.schema().auth.password && !engine.schema().auth.guests)) { Ok(()) }
        else { Err(dev_doors::closed()) }
    }
    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 { lock(&self.clock).now().max(self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire)) }
    pub(crate) fn refresh_time_audience_clock(&self, engine: &Engine, now: i64) {
        if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
            let durable = engine.store().meta("time_audience_clock").ok().flatten()
                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN);
            self.time_audience_clock.fetch_max(now.max(durable), Ordering::AcqRel);
        } else {
            self.time_audience_clock.store(i64::MIN, Ordering::Release);
        }
    }
    fn advance_time_audiences(&self) -> Result<i64, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        let requested = lock(&self.clock).now();
        // Non-timed reader traffic must retain its independent reader lane,
        // including while adoption or a mutation deliberately holds the writer.
        if self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire) == i64::MIN { return Ok(requested); }
        let mut engine = lock(&self.engine);
        let before = engine.seq()?;
        let now = engine.advance_time_audiences_at(requested)?;

/bin/bash -lc 'timeout 10s cat snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/activation_walk.rs snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/activation_scan.rs' in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-mig
