   338	        }
   339	    }
   340	    Ok(out)
   381	fn historical_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   382	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   383	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   384	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   385	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   386	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   387	        let Some(data)=data else {continue};
   388	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   389	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   390	        let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid historical row"))};
   391	        if let Value::Row(inputs)|Value::Map(inputs)=&e.inputs {for(c,value)in inputs {
   392	            if e.absent.contains(c) || !fields.contains_key(c) || fields.get(c)==Some(value) {
   393	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   394	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   395	                if e.absent.contains(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{fields.entry(c.clone()).or_insert(value.clone());}
   396	            }
   397	        }}
   398	        for c in e.after.keys() {if !e.before.contains_key(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}}
   399	        for(c,value)in e.before {fields.insert(c,value);}
   400	        // Keep the receipt's omitted-input meaning separate from its later
   401	        // rollback output preimage/default.
   402	        for c in &e.absent {if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}}
   403	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   404	    } Ok(())
   405	}
   406	fn rebuild_reads(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,reads:&BTreeSet<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   407	    for (name,index) in reads {
   408	        let old=&source.tables[name].indexes[index];let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;
   409	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   410	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,mut row|{
   411	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   412	            for key in index_keys(old,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   413	        })?;
   414	        if old.unique {validate_lookup(tx,ti,old.components.len(),name,index)?;}
   415	    } Ok(())
   508	    } Ok(())
   509	}

   511	pub(super) fn record_omissions(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   512	    if !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   513	    let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   514	    for c in columns {if row.field(&c).is_err(){note_omission(tx,t,id,&c)?;}}Ok(())
   515	}

   517	fn input_fingerprint<'a>(row:&Value,names:impl Iterator<Item=&'a str>)->String {
   518	    let fields:BTreeMap<String,Value>=names.map(|c|(c.to_owned(),row.field(c).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null))).collect();
   519	    crate::value::digest(&fields)
   520	}

   522	fn note_omission(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,c:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   523	    // A provided historical cell stays provided when merely moved out of the
   524	    // active projection. Already-recorded omission survives default freezing.
   525	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_absence SELECT ?1,?2,'',?3 WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,c])?;Ok(())
   526	}
   527	fn source_value(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value,column:&str,table:Option<&Table>,meter:&mut Meter)->Result<Option<Value>,RuntimeError>{
   528	    if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){meter.heap(value.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;return Ok(Some(value.clone()));}
   529	    if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {return Ok(None);}
   530	    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   531	    meter.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0).max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   532	    let text:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   533	    if let Some(text)=text{return Ok(Some(decode(&text)?));}
   534	    Ok(table.and_then(|table|{
   535	        if table.maintained.contains_key(column){Some(Value::Int(0))}
   536	        else if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind|kind.is_optional()){Some(Value::Null)}
   537	        else {table.defaults.get(column).and_then(|d|if let crate::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(v)=d {Some(v.clone())}else{None})}
   538	    }))
   539	}
   540	fn native_inputs<'a>(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value,names:impl Iterator<Item=&'a str>,table:Option<&Table>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,RuntimeError>{
   541	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   542	    for c in names {out.insert(c.to_owned(),source_value(tx,t,id,row,c,table,&mut meter)?.unwrap_or(Value::Null));}Ok(out)
   543	}

//! Writes: the four words, their rules, the constraints (unique, cap,
//! immutable, append-only, after-insert), maintained aggregates, and the
//! partition events every row change produces.

use super::runner::Runner;
use super::validate::validate_row;
use crate::error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError};
use crate::partition;
use crate::rows::index_values;
use crate::rows::{Bounds, Dir};
use crate::schema::{Constraint, Effect, Table, materialize_optional_fields};
use crate::value::{Supplied, Value, canonical_json};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Origin {
    Source,
    Maintained,
    LiveAuthority,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub(super) struct FinalExistsWitness {
    table: String,
    row: Value,
    constraint: usize,
}

#[path = "write_references.rs"]
mod references;
#[path = "write_target.rs"]
pub(crate) mod target;

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn insert(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.insert_mode(table, row, false).map(|_| ())
    }

    pub(super) fn insert_if_absent(&mut self, table: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.insert_mode(table, row, true)
    }

    fn insert_mode(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.device && self.schema.tables[table_name].not_before.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "a mutation touching a `not before` table runs on the server".into(),
            ));
        }
        if self.device && if_absent && self.schema.tables[table_name].through.is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("insert if absent on a read-through child queues without a row".into()));
        }
        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
            let mark = runner.meter.mark();
            let result = runner.insert_attempt(table_name, row, if_absent, None).map_err(|error| error.authorization_context(table_name, Effect::Insert));
            // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — denied direct allocations hide deferred
            // read witnesses and counter work just as NOT_FOUND hides targets.
            if matches!(&result, Err(RuntimeError::Rule { effect: Effect::Insert, .. } | RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { .. }))
                && runner.schema.tables.get(table_name).is_some_and(|table| !table.sequences.is_empty()) {
                runner.meter.restore(mark);
            }
            result
        })
    }

    fn insert_attempt(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied, if_absent: bool, upsert_index: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Insert)?;
        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, &row, true)?;
        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, &row)?;
        let supplied_sequence = super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, &row);
        // A directly supplied field is a payload error before any lookup;
        // only an upsert's copied address waits for insert authority.
        if upsert_index.is_none() && supplied_sequence.is_err() { return supplied_sequence.map(|_| false); }
        let supplied = row.clone();
        let row = self.with_defaults(table_name, row)?;
        if self.schema.tables[table_name].ephemeral.is_some() { self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?; }
        let row = row.ephemeral(&self.schema.tables[table_name], self.context.now.unwrap_or(0))?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — reserve, then stamp the parent capture before any rule sees the image.
        self.reserve_capture(&self.schema.tables[table_name])?;
        let row = self.capture_insert(table_name, row)?;
        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        let independent = !table.sequences.is_empty() && !super::rules::deciding_fields(table_name, table, &table.rules.insert)
            .iter().any(|field| table.sequences.contains_key(field));
        // An independent denial must never consult a hidden read witness.
        let authorized = independent && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)?;
        if independent && !authorized {
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
        }
        if !table.sequences.is_empty() {
            // Admission reserves the worst case before either read verdict;
            // restoring a refusal alone cannot hide a boundary abort.
            // An independent insert rule was already prepaid and evaluated.
            // Only still-pending checks belong in this preflight allowance.
            let insert_checks = if authorized { 0 } else { 1 + u64::from(upsert_index.is_some() && supplied_sequence.is_ok()) };
            self.reserve_sequence_authorization(table, &row, insert_checks)?;
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — an unreadable allocation cannot expose counter,
        // uniqueness or cap state, even to a principal allowed to insert.
        if !self.sequence_insert_readable(table_name, &row)? {
            self.refusing_clause(table_name, "sequence read authority");
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "sequence read authority") });
        }
    }

    /// A column with a declared default takes it when the written row leaves
    /// it out, before validation and before the asset facts, so the row the
    /// rules and the constraints see is the complete one.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.4 — omitted defaults use the clock and viewer of the host running the insert.
    pub(super) fn with_defaults(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        if !table.defaults.keys().any(|name| {
            row.as_object().is_ok_and(|fields| !fields.contains_key(name))
        }) {
            return Ok(row);
        }
        let row = row.defaults(table,self.context.now.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Input("write clock is not pinned".into()))?,&self.context.viewer)?;
        self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes())?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// An `image`/`video` column names an upload by id; the row carries the
    /// facts the server established about it (type, bytes, dimensions,
    /// duration), never the client's word for them. The upload must be the
    /// writer's own and of the column's kind and size. The system principal
    /// may name an asset that was never uploaded: a fixture's placeholder,
    /// metadata only, never served.
    // Web stores hold received asset facts, never the authoritative upload ledger.
    #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
    fn with_asset_facts(&mut self, _table_name: &str, row: Value, _old: Option<&Value>, _hidden_patch: Option<&Value>) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(row)
    }

    pub(super) fn refuse_upsert_owned(&self, table: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table, Effect::Insert)?;
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table, Effect::Update)
    }

    /// Programs never write a maintained target; the engine does.
    pub(super) fn refuse_engine_owned(&self, table: &str, effect: Effect) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — pending member changes cannot rewrite settled requirements.
        if self.device && effect != Effect::Insert && self.schema.receipts.iter().any(|f| f.members == table || f.runs == table) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("receipt membership changes and progress queue until settlement".into()));
        }
        self.refuse_receipt_table(table)?;
        self.refuse_condition_write()?;
        self.refuse_timeline_write(table, effect)?;
        self.schema.refuse_private(table)?;
        if self.schema.maintain_for(table).is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{table} is a maintained aggregate; write its source rows instead")));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn check_constraints(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: &Value, effect: Effect) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).expect("validated");
        let id = new.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#4.1 — an action's own write consults no rule: probing the child's read rule would draw the shared allowance only where a child exists.
        let readable = match old { Some(old) if effect == Effect::Update && !self.engine_action => {
                !self.inactive_write_target(table_name, old)?
                    && self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Read, None, old)?
            }
            _ => true };
        if effect != Effect::Delete {
            for (name, index) in table.indexes.iter().filter(|(_, index)| index.unique) {
                // byId can only return this id (already checked on insert).
                // An unchanged admitted unique key cannot acquire a collision.
                if name == "byId" { continue; }
                let key = index_values(index, new);
                if old.is_some_and(|old| index_values(index, old) == key) { continue; }
                if key.iter().any(|value| *value == Value::Null) {
                    continue;
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — holding the target does not cover a different unique population.
                if self.device && effect == Effect::Update && !self.device_work(|runner| {
                        runner.upsert_absence_proven(table_name, name, &key)
                    })? {
                    return Err(self.write_miss(table_name, name, &key));
                }
                if let Some(existing) = self.collision_lookup(table_name, name, index, &key, Some(&id), effect)
                    .map_err(|error| {
                        if !readable && matches!(error, RuntimeError::Rule { .. }) { self.no_write_target() } else { error
                        }
                    })?
                    && existing.field("id")?.as_str()? != id
                {
                    self.observe_sequence_columns(table_name, new, &index.columns(), effect == Effect::Insert)?;
                    if effect == Effect::Update && !readable {
                        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                        self.refusing_clause(table_name, &format!("unique {}", index.columns().join(", ")));
                        return Err(self.no_write_target());
                    }
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.9 — the dev log names the `unique` line that refused (wave 3 I3).
                    self.refusing_clause(table_name, &format!("unique {}", index.columns().join(", ")));
    for fill in definitions.iter().rev().filter(|f|!next_keys.contains(&f.key())) {
        let Some(table)=next.table(&fill.table) else {continue};
        let t=tx.num(&fill.table)?;let key=fill.key();let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();let mut count=0;
        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
            let Some(mut e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&key)? else {
                let pruned:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3 AND active=1)",params![t,id,key],|r|r.get(0))?;
                if pruned && fill.outputs.iter().any(|c|table.columns.contains_key(c)) {bad.add(id,"rollback preimage was explicitly pruned; select a bridge preserving current data or restore a backup");}
                return Ok(())
            };
            if !e.active && e.retained.is_empty() {return Ok(())}
            let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid stored row"))};
            let mut changed=false;
            for c in &fill.outputs {
                if !e.active && !e.retained.contains(c){continue;}
                if !table.columns.contains_key(c){e.retained.insert(c.clone());continue;}

                let current=fields.get(c);let before=e.before.get(c);let after=e.after.get(c);
                if current!=after && current!=before {bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} changed after backfill {}; keep a bridge and repair through an authorized mutation before rollback",fill.table,c,fill.name));return Ok(())}
                if let Some(value)=before {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{
                    fields.remove(c);
                    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_unprovided VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,id,c])?;
                }
                e.retained.remove(c);
                changed=true;
            }
            if changed {tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;count+=1;}
            save(tx,t,id,&key,&e,false)
        })?;

NOT READY

- **[P1] Lookup reads bypass inverse-absence semantics.** [backfills.rs:411](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-mig/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/backfills.rs:411) rebuilds indexes using ordinary default materialization; lookup evaluation does likewise. Only `source_value` checks `_backfill_unprovided`. When leaving a transform that added `score: int default 10`, reversal removes the originally absent cell. A replacement declaration can then read null through its historical `score` argument but 10 through a point lookup of that same row, silently committing inconsistent results. Apply the absence mask consistently to arguments, lookup images, and index construction. Add a regression comparing direct and lookup reads during replacement.

- **[P1] Pruning forgets suspended output reversal obligations.** [backfills.rs:322](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-mig/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/backfills.rs:322) preserves only the receipt’s `active` flag, losing `retained_outputs`; reversal discovers only active pruned identities. Transform an existing optional column, remove that column and declaration while retaining the row, then prune. Selecting the original plain schema now succeeds with null instead of refusing the lost preimage. Preserve per-output reversal-unavailable membership for inactive receipts too, and check it whenever a plain schema exposes those outputs.
